tatra prathamo'dhyāyaḥ |
aiśamādyantanirmuktamatiśobhanamādarāva ||
namāmi vigrahaṃ sāmvaṃ saṃsāraviṣabheṣajam || 1 ||
yadyapi gatakhaṇḍe jñānasya svarūpaṃ muktisādhanatā coktā tathā'pi-
"
yajñaśiṣṭāmṛtabhujo yānti brahma sanātanam |
nāyaṃ loko'styayajñasya kuto'nyaḥ kurusattama "
ityādibhagavadvacanādyajñaśabdasya kriyābhede kevalama siddhidarśanāttadvirodhena
jñāne mābhūdanādara iti jñānasya yajñarūpatā karmayajñebhyaḥ śreporūpatā ca vaktavyā |
uktā ca saṃpraheṇa bhagavatā -
" śreyāndravyamapādyajñājjñānayajñaḥ paraṃtapa " iti |
sā'pi nirūpayitavyeti yajñavaibhavakhaṇḍaṃ caturthamārabhamāṇo bhagavānvādarā-
yaṇaḥ prathamataḥ parāparaśivapraṇidhānamupanivabhāti - aiśamiti | atra prathamāyeṃ
nityaniratiśayānandarūpatvena niṣkalasya praṇidhānaṃ dvitīye ca paramapuruṣārtha-
madatvena sakalasya praṇidhānaṃ namāmīti trikaraṇavyāpārasya tatra samarpaṇaṃ copa-
nibadhyate | padeśaṃ niṣkalaṃ śarīraṃ svarūpaṃ tadeva lokānugrahāya svīkṛtalīlāva-
tāraṃ satsāmbaṃ namāmīti saṃbandhaḥ || 1 ||
satrāvasāne saṃnadvāḥ sarvavedārthavedane ||
sarvalokahite yuktāḥ sārāsāravivekinaḥ || 2 ||
sarvavedārtheti | paravedārthavadaparavedārthasyāpi vedane sūtamukhāduktakrameṇa parave-
dārthaṃ jñātvā kṛtakṛtyānāmapi munīnāṃ punaraparavedārthapraśne prayojanaṃ paravidyāna -
dhikāriṇāṃ karmayajñadvārā vadadhikāramasiddhiyā syāditi lokānugraha eva
prayojanamityāha - sarvalokahita iti | nigadvyākhyāto'dhyāyaśeṣaḥ || 2 ||
svādhyāyādhyayane yuktāḥ svasthacittāḥ suniścalāḥ ||
sarvopadravanirmuktāḥ sarvaśatruvivarjitāḥ || 3 ||

tadevā''ha - vedārthaḥ parama iti | parātsakalādatiśayena yatparaṃ niṣkalaṃ tadukta-

miti hi sthitiḥ | nanu sarvajñe niravadye śive nājñānaṃ saṃbhavati na cājhe jīve
bhāṣādhīno jīvavibhāgo jīvāśrapā ca māyetyanyonyāśrayādiprasaṅgāditpata
āha - cinmātreti | iha bhūtale ghaṭa iti hi nirghaṭasya bhūtalasya ghaṭa saṃbandhavyā-
pārātsvātmanaiva ghaṭavati tasmiṃstatsaṃbandha ātmāśrayādvitīye ghaṭavatyanyonyā-

saṃvandhaḥ | evaṃ māpātadabhāvasādhāraṇe cinmātre māpā''śriteti || 7 ||
adhiṣṭhānāvaśeṣo hi nāśaḥ kalpitavastunaḥ ||
bhāvasyaiva hyabhāvatvaṃ nāśo'bhāvasya bhavataḥ ||
bhāvābhāvasvabhāvābhyāmanya eva hi kelpitaḥ || 8 ||

uktāṃnumāna upādhimāśaṅkate -- kāryasyeti | ghaṭapaṭādeḥ kṛtakatvādanityatvaṃ
na tvanātmatvāt | ato vipadāderanityatvaṃ kathaṃ syādityarthaḥ | apamāśayaḥ |
anityatvalakṣaṇe sādhye kṛtakatvamupādhiḥ | yatrānātmatvaṃ tatra kṛtakatvamiti
sādhanavyāptirnāsti | māyāyāṃ prāgabhāvādiṣvanātmatvamasti kṛtakatvābhāvāt |
ataḥ sādhanāvyāpakatvaṃ dṛṣṭānte sarvatra | patrānityatvaṃ tatra kṛtakatvamiti
vyāptisadbhāvātsādhyavyāpakatvam | sādhanāvyāpakatve sati sādhyasya vyāpaka-
tvamiti hi talakṣaṇam | tasmādanātmakatvādanityatvaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ |
sādhyavyāpakatvābhāvenopādhiṃ dūṣayati--na tadyuktamiti | ghaṭaprāgabhāvasthā-
nityatve satyapi tasyānāditvena kṛtakatvābhāvātsādhyā vyāpakatvamityarthaḥ |

danityatvadarśanāt | ghaṭabhāgabhāvasya hi ghaṭotpatti nivartyasyānityatvaṃ sarvairapya-

atha sodhpa viśiṣṭopādheḥ sādhyavyāpakatvamupapādayati-- bhāvarūpaspeti |
bhāvatve satyanityatvaṃ tatkṛtakatvaṃ na vyabhicaratīti sādhyavyāpakaṃ kṛtakatvame-

[ 4 yajñavaibhavakhaṇḍe -
-
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dravyaśuddhiṃ samāsataḥ ||
śraddhayā saha viprendrāḥ śṛṇutā''tmaviśuddhaye || 1 ||
sacidānandarūpasyā''tmanaḥ svābhāvikaśuddhiṃ siddhavatkṛtya tadupajīvanena
tatsvarūpe'dhyastā vidyātatkāryavilasitarūpasya dopajātasya nivartakamāyaścitta-
muktam | atha tāṃ svataḥśuddhimātmanaḥ pratipādayituṃ dravyaśuddhiṃ pratijānīte
athāta iti || 1 ||
-
ātmānātmeti viprendrā dvidhā dravyaṃ vyavasthitam ||
ātmadravyaṃ svayaṃ śuddhaṃ satyajñānādilakṣaṇam || 2 ||
dravyaṃ vyavasthitamiti | dravyaśabdo vastuparyāyo na tu parābhimatadravyatvajā-
tipravṛttinimittakastathātve tvātmano dravyatvajātisaṃsargaviraheṇa dravyaśabdavā-

hiyate | uktaṃ dyācāryaiḥ - " ānando viṣayānubhavo nityatvaṃ ceti santi dharmāḥ |
apṛthaktve'pi caitanyātpṛthagivāvabhāsante" iti || 2 ||
sattāmātre na doṣo'sti svataḥ śuddhaṃ yataḥ sadā ||
yadi sattā svato'śuddhā duṣṭaṃ sarve bhavetsadā || 3 ||

meva sattetyucyate | tasya sattāmātrasya yasmātsvata: śuddhiraṅgīkāryā | atastatra
na dopasparśa ityarthaḥ | vipakṣe bādhakamāha - yadi satteti | svata iti | kāraṇā-
ntaramanapekṣya svarūpata evetyarthaḥ || 3 ||
duṣṭasattābhisaṃbandhāduṣṭaspṛṣṭaṃ sadā khalu ||

asya ca sākṣicaitanyasphuraṇasya vṛtijñānato viśeṣamāha - bhānamiti | vṛtti-
jñānaṃ hi jaḍatvātsākṣicaitanyarūpeṇa bhānāntareṇaiva bhāti | sākṣirūpaṃ bhānaṃ
naiva bhānāntareṇāpi tu svayameva prakāśate | prakāśamānaṃ hi jñānamarthasya bhāsakaṃ
tatprakāśanaṃ ca jñānāntarāyattaṃ cettatrāpyevamityanavasthā syāttato jñānasya jñānā-
ntaravedyatvaṃ na saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ | sati hi jñānāntare tadvedyatvaṃ jñānasyā''śakyeta
tadeva nāstītyāha- mānarūpeṇeti | ghaṭapaṭādidṛśyabhedopādhinā pāhagrūpaṃ jñānaṃ
vibhinnaṃ pratīyate taddṛśyasaṃbandhaṃ parityajya kevalaṃ sphuraṇarūpeṇa sarvadaikameva na tu
bhinnam | ghaṭapaṭādijñāneṣu sphuraṇamātrasyāviśeṣeṇa pratyabhijñāyamānatvādityarthaḥ
|| 88 ||
bhātaṃ ānaṃ sadā svena bhāsā bhāti na cānyataḥ ||
yatsvayaṃ bhāti tadvānaṃ khalu bhosyasya bhāsakam || 45 ||
evaṃ bhānasyaikatvena tadgocaraṃ sphuraṇāntaraṃ nāsti cetkathaṃ tarhi tasya patī-
tirityata āha- bhātamiti | pratīyamānaṃ sphuraṇarūpaṃ sākṣicaitanyaṃ svenaiva bhāsā
bhāti na cānyasmātprakāśāntarāt | śrūyate hi "na tatra sūryo bhāti na candra-
tārakaṃ nemā vidyuto bhānti kuto'yamagniḥ | tameva bhāntamanu bhāti sarvaṃ taspa
bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhāti " iti | svayaṃprakāśamānasyaiva prakāśakatvaṃ loke prasi-
ddhamiti darśayati-yatsvayamiti | yatprabhādīpādikaṃ sajātīyaprakāśāntaranairape-
kṣyeṇa bhāsate tasya khalu loke prakāśakatvaṃ dṛṣṭam | ato jñānaṃ jñānāntaranairape-
kṣyeṇa svayaṃprakāśamānameva saṃgrāsyaṃ viṣayaṃ prakāśayet | tasya jñānāntaravedyatve'-
navasthāprasakteḥ | ye tu cakṣurādivannilīnameva vijñānaṃ viṣaye jñātatārūpaṃ phalaṃ
janayatīti varṇayanti tairapi viṣayasphuraṇarūpā jñātatā svaprakāśatvenaiṣṭavyā |
tathā sati saiva cakṣurādikaraṇajanyā'stu kṛtamantargaḍunā jñānena || 45 ||
ataḥ sarvasya sākṣyasya sākṣibhūtaḥ svayaṃprabhaḥ ||
ātmā sākṣyagatairdharmaiḥ karmabhirna sa badhyate || 46 ||
evamadvitīyasvaprakāśacinmātrarūpatvamātmanaḥ prasādhya sarvasākṣitayā sākṣyā-
nanupraviṣṭasya pratijñātaṃ sākṣyagata karmasaṃbandhābhāvaṃ nigamayati-ata iti || 46 ||
grūpaṃ dṛśyasaṃvaddhamiti vaktuṃ na śakyate || 47 ||
